===== FILE: crates/yellow_trellis_0001/src/lib.rs =====
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Sample {
    pub sequence: u32,
    pub magnitude: u64,
    pub complete: bool,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Totals {
    pub complete: usize,
    pub incomplete: usize,
    pub magnitude: u64,
}

pub fn fold_yellow_trellis_0001(samples: &[Sample]) -> Result<Totals, &'static str> {
    if samples.len() > 395 {
        return Err("sample collection exceeds the synthetic bound");
    }
    let mut totals = Totals { complete: 0, incomplete: 0, magnitude: 0 };
    for sample in samples {
        totals.magnitude = totals.magnitude
            .checked_add(sample.magnitude)
            .ok_or("magnitude overflow")?;
        if sample.complete {
            totals.complete += 1;
        } else {
            totals.incomplete += 1;
        }
    }
    Ok(totals)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn incomplete_samples_remain_in_totals() {
        let samples = [
            Sample { sequence: 1, magnitude: 8, complete: true },
            Sample { sequence: 2, magnitude: 13, complete: false },
        ];
        let totals = fold_yellow_trellis_0001(&samples).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(totals.complete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.incomplete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.magnitude, 21);
    }
}

===== FILE: web/linen_registry_0002.ts =====
export type Observation = Readonly<{
  sequence: number;
  category: "calm" | "changing" | "uncertain";
  reading: number;
}>;

export type WindowSummary = Readonly<{
  count: number;
  minimum: number | null;
  maximum: number | null;
  span: number | null;
}>;

export function summarizeLinenRegistry0002(
  rows: readonly Observation[],
): WindowSummary {
  if (rows.length === 0) {
    return { count: 0, minimum: null, maximum: null, span: null };
  }
  if (rows.length > 128) {
    throw new Error("synthetic observation window is too wide");
  }
  let minimum = rows[0].reading;
  let maximum = rows[0].reading;
  for (const row of rows.slice(1)) {
    minimum = Math.min(minimum, row.reading);
    maximum = Math.max(maximum, row.reading);
  }
  return { count: rows.length, minimum, maximum, span: maximum - minimum };
}

# Design record: umber atlas 0006

The working record says that a small observatory crew maps several competing explanations. It does so while preserving the original ordering of evidence.
The morning logistics crew measures the remaining design alternatives after comparing the calm and windy trial periods.

===== FILE: modules/willow_folio_0009.py =====
"""Synthetic readiness aggregation for the fictional willow_folio_0009 module."""

from dataclasses import dataclass


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Reading:
    label: str
    value: int
    accepted: bool


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Summary:
    accepted: int
    rejected: int
    total: int


def summarize_willow_folio_0009(readings: list[Reading], ceiling: int = 54) -> Summary:
    """Classify bounded mock readings without discarding rejected rows."""
    if ceiling < 1:
        raise ValueError("ceiling must be positive")
    accepted = 0
    rejected = 0
    total = 0
    for reading in readings:
        if reading.value < 0:
            raise ValueError("reading values must be non-negative")
        total += reading.value
        if reading.accepted and reading.value <= ceiling:
            accepted += 1
        else:
            rejected += 1
    return Summary(accepted=accepted, rejected=rejected, total=total)


def render_willow_folio_0009(summary: Summary) -> str:
    fields = (
        f"accepted={summary.accepted}",
        f"rejected={summary.rejected}",
        f"total={summary.total}",
    )
    return ", ".join(fields)

===== FILE: web/north_matrix_0011.ts =====
export type Observation = Readonly<{
  sequence: number;
  category: "calm" | "changing" | "uncertain";
  reading: number;
}>;

export type WindowSummary = Readonly<{
  count: number;
  minimum: number | null;
  maximum: number | null;
  span: number | null;
}>;

export function summarizeNorthMatrix0011(
  rows: readonly Observation[],
): WindowSummary {
  if (rows.length === 0) {
    return { count: 0, minimum: null, maximum: null, span: null };
  }
  if (rows.length > 416) {
    throw new Error("synthetic observation window is too wide");
  }
  let minimum = rows[0].reading;
  let maximum = rows[0].reading;
  for (const row of rows.slice(1)) {
    minimum = Math.min(minimum, row.reading);
    maximum = Math.max(maximum, row.reading);
  }
  return { count: rows.length, minimum, maximum, span: maximum - minimum };
}

===== FILE: migrations/0014_river_engine_0014.sql =====
BEGIN;

CREATE TABLE river_engine_0014_observations (
    sequence INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
    category TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (category IN ('calm', 'changing', 'uncertain')),
    reading INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (reading >= 0),
    unit TEXT NOT NULL,
    complete INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (complete IN (0, 1))
);

CREATE INDEX river_engine_0014_category_sequence
    ON river_engine_0014_observations(category, sequence);

INSERT INTO river_engine_0014_observations(sequence, category, reading, unit, complete) VALUES
    (1, 'calm', 77, 'points', 1),
    (2, 'changing', 770, 'samples', 1),
    (3, 'uncertain', 194, 'minutes', 0),
    (4, 'calm', 48, 'points', 1);

COMMIT;

===== FILE: crates/harbor_ledger_0019/src/lib.rs =====
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Sample {
    pub sequence: u32,
    pub magnitude: u64,
    pub complete: bool,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Totals {
    pub complete: usize,
    pub incomplete: usize,
    pub magnitude: u64,
}

pub fn fold_harbor_ledger_0019(samples: &[Sample]) -> Result<Totals, &'static str> {
    if samples.len() > 370 {
        return Err("sample collection exceeds the synthetic bound");
    }
    let mut totals = Totals { complete: 0, incomplete: 0, magnitude: 0 };
    for sample in samples {
        totals.magnitude = totals.magnitude
            .checked_add(sample.magnitude)
            .ok_or("magnitude overflow")?;
        if sample.complete {
            totals.complete += 1;
        } else {
            totals.incomplete += 1;
        }
    }
    Ok(totals)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn incomplete_samples_remain_in_totals() {
        let samples = [
            Sample { sequence: 1, magnitude: 8, complete: true },
            Sample { sequence: 2, magnitude: 13, complete: false },
        ];
        let totals = fold_harbor_ledger_0019(&samples).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(totals.complete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.incomplete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.magnitude, 21);
    }
}

===== FILE: modules/yellow_ledger_0027.py =====
"""Synthetic stability aggregation for the fictional yellow_ledger_0027 module."""

from dataclasses import dataclass


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Reading:
    label: str
    value: int
    accepted: bool


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Summary:
    accepted: int
    rejected: int
    total: int


def summarize_yellow_ledger_0027(readings: list[Reading], ceiling: int = 121) -> Summary:
    """Classify bounded mock readings without discarding rejected rows."""
    if ceiling < 1:
        raise ValueError("ceiling must be positive")
    accepted = 0
    rejected = 0
    total = 0
    for reading in readings:
        if reading.value < 0:
            raise ValueError("reading values must be non-negative")
        total += reading.value
        if reading.accepted and reading.value <= ceiling:
            accepted += 1
        else:
            rejected += 1
    return Summary(accepted=accepted, rejected=rejected, total=total)


def render_yellow_ledger_0027(summary: Summary) -> str:
    fields = (
        f"accepted={summary.accepted}",
        f"rejected={summary.rejected}",
        f"total={summary.total}",
    )
    return ", ".join(fields)

===== FILE: web/velvet_matrix_0029.ts =====
export type Observation = Readonly<{
  sequence: number;
  category: "calm" | "changing" | "uncertain";
  reading: number;
}>;

export type WindowSummary = Readonly<{
  count: number;
  minimum: number | null;
  maximum: number | null;
  span: number | null;
}>;

export function summarizeVelvetMatrix0029(
  rows: readonly Observation[],
): WindowSummary {
  if (rows.length === 0) {
    return { count: 0, minimum: null, maximum: null, span: null };
  }
  if (rows.length > 608) {
    throw new Error("synthetic observation window is too wide");
  }
  let minimum = rows[0].reading;
  let maximum = rows[0].reading;
  for (const row of rows.slice(1)) {
    minimum = Math.min(minimum, row.reading);
    maximum = Math.max(maximum, row.reading);
  }
  return { count: rows.length, minimum, maximum, span: maximum - minimum };
}

    for reading in readings:
        if reading.value < 0:
            raise ValueError("reading values must be non-negative")
        total += reading.value
        if reading.accepted and reading.value <= ceiling:
            accepted += 1
        else:
            rejected += 1
    return Summary(accepted=accepted, rejected=rejected, total=total)


def render_indigo_ledger_0036(summary: Summary) -> str:
    fields = (
        f"accepted={summary.accepted}",
        f"rejected={summary.rejected}",
        f"total={summary.total}",
    )
    return ", ".join(fields)

===== FILE: crates/drift_trellis_0037/src/lib.rs =====
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Sample {
    pub sequence: u32,
    pub magnitude: u64,
    pub complete: bool,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Totals {
    pub complete: usize,
    pub incomplete: usize,
    pub magnitude: u64,
}

pub fn fold_drift_trellis_0037(samples: &[Sample]) -> Result<Totals, &'static str> {
    if samples.len() > 381 {
        return Err("sample collection exceeds the synthetic bound");
    }
    let mut totals = Totals { complete: 0, incomplete: 0, magnitude: 0 };
    for sample in samples {
        totals.magnitude = totals.magnitude
            .checked_add(sample.magnitude)
            .ok_or("magnitude overflow")?;
        if sample.complete {
            totals.complete += 1;
        } else {
            totals.incomplete += 1;
        }
    }
    Ok(totals)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn incomplete_samples_remain_in_totals() {
        let samples = [
            Sample { sequence: 1, magnitude: 8, complete: true },
            Sample { sequence: 2, magnitude: 13, complete: false },
        ];
        let totals = fold_drift_trellis_0037(&samples).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(totals.complete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.incomplete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.magnitude, 21);
    }
}

    pub incomplete: usize,
    pub magnitude: u64,
}

pub fn fold_yellow_signal_0046(samples: &[Sample]) -> Result<Totals, &'static str> {
    if samples.len() > 493 {
        return Err("sample collection exceeds the synthetic bound");
    }
    let mut totals = Totals { complete: 0, incomplete: 0, magnitude: 0 };

        totals.magnitude = totals.magnitude

}

===== FILE: web/cedar_harvest_0047.ts =====
export type Observation = Readonly<{
  sequence: number;
  category: "calm" | "changing" | "uncertain";
  reading: number;
}>;

export type WindowSummary = Readonly<{
  count: number;
  minimum: number | null;
  maximum: number | null;
  span: number | null;
}>;

export function summarizeCedarHarvest0047(
  rows: readonly Observation[],
): WindowSummary {
  if (rows.length === 0) {
    return { count: 0, minimum: null, maximum: null, span: null };
  }
  if (rows.length > 288) {
    throw new Error("synthetic observation window is too wide");
  }
  let minimum = rows[0].reading;
  let maximum = rows[0].reading;
  for (const row of rows.slice(1)) {
    minimum = Math.min(minimum, row.reading);
    maximum = Math.max(maximum, row.reading);
  }
  return { count: rows.length, minimum, maximum, span: maximum - minimum };
}

===== FILE: modules/meadow_trellis_0054.py =====
"""Synthetic noise aggregation for the fictional meadow_trellis_0054 module."""

from dataclasses import dataclass


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Reading:
    label: str
    value: int
    accepted: bool


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Summary:
    accepted: int
    rejected: int
    total: int


def summarize_meadow_trellis_0054(readings: list[Reading], ceiling: int = 54) -> Summary:
    """Classify bounded mock readings without discarding rejected rows."""
    if ceiling < 1:
        raise ValueError("ceiling must be positive")
    accepted = 0
    rejected = 0
    total = 0
    for reading in readings:
        if reading.value < 0:
            raise ValueError("reading values must be non-negative")
        total += reading.value
        if reading.accepted and reading.value <= ceiling:
            accepted += 1
        else:
            rejected += 1
    return Summary(accepted=accepted, rejected=rejected, total=total)


def render_meadow_trellis_0054(summary: Summary) -> str:
    fields = (
        f"accepted={summary.accepted}",
        f"rejected={summary.rejected}",
        f"total={summary.total}",
    )
    return ", ".join(fields)

===== FILE: web/evening_delta_0056.ts =====
export type Observation = Readonly<{
  sequence: number;
  category: "calm" | "changing" | "uncertain";
  reading: number;
}>;

export type WindowSummary = Readonly<{
  count: number;
  minimum: number | null;
  maximum: number | null;
  span: number | null;
}>;

export function summarizeEveningDelta0056(
  rows: readonly Observation[],
): WindowSummary {
  if (rows.length === 0) {
    return { count: 0, minimum: null, maximum: null, span: null };
  }
  if (rows.length > 352) {
    throw new Error("synthetic observation window is too wide");
  }
  let minimum = rows[0].reading;
  let maximum = rows[0].reading;
  for (const row of rows.slice(1)) {
    minimum = Math.min(minimum, row.reading);
    maximum = Math.max(maximum, row.reading);
  }
  return { count: rows.length, minimum, maximum, span: maximum - minimum };
}

===== FILE: crates/quiet_matrix_0064/src/lib.rs =====
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Sample {
    pub sequence: u32,
    pub magnitude: u64,
    pub complete: bool,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Totals {
    pub complete: usize,
    pub incomplete: usize,
    pub magnitude: u64,
}

pub fn fold_quiet_matrix_0064(samples: &[Sample]) -> Result<Totals, &'static str> {
    if samples.len() > 431 {
        return Err("sample collection exceeds the synthetic bound");
    }
    let mut totals = Totals { complete: 0, incomplete: 0, magnitude: 0 };
    for sample in samples {
        totals.magnitude = totals.magnitude
            .checked_add(sample.magnitude)
            .ok_or("magnitude overflow")?;
        if sample.complete {
            totals.complete += 1;
        } else {
            totals.incomplete += 1;
        }
    }
    Ok(totals)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn incomplete_samples_remain_in_totals() {
        let samples = [
            Sample { sequence: 1, magnitude: 8, complete: true },
            Sample { sequence: 2, magnitude: 13, complete: false },
        ];
        let totals = fold_quiet_matrix_0064(&samples).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(totals.complete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.incomplete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.magnitude, 21);
    }
}

===== FILE: modules/velvet_folio_0072.py =====
"""Synthetic coverage aggregation for the fictional velvet_folio_0072 module."""

from dataclasses import dataclass


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Reading:
    label: str
    value: int
    accepted: bool


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Summary:
    accepted: int
    rejected: int
    total: int


def summarize_velvet_folio_0072(readings: list[Reading], ceiling: int = 24) -> Summary:
    """Classify bounded mock readings without discarding rejected rows."""
    if ceiling < 1:
        raise ValueError("ceiling must be positive")
    accepted = 0
    rejected = 0
    total = 0
    for reading in readings:
        if reading.value < 0:
            raise ValueError("reading values must be non-negative")
        total += reading.value
        if reading.accepted and reading.value <= ceiling:
            accepted += 1
        else:
            rejected += 1
    return Summary(accepted=accepted, rejected=rejected, total=total)


def render_velvet_folio_0072(summary: Summary) -> str:
    fields = (
        f"accepted={summary.accepted}",
        f"rejected={summary.rejected}",
        f"total={summary.total}",
    )

===== FILE: web/tidal_atlas_0074.ts =====
export type Observation = Readonly<{
  sequence: number;
  category: "calm" | "changing" | "uncertain";
  reading: number;
}>;

export type WindowSummary = Readonly<{
  count: number;
  minimum: number | null;
  maximum: number | null;
  span: number | null;
}>;

export function summarizeTidalAtlas0074(
  rows: readonly Observation[],
): WindowSummary {
  if (rows.length === 0) {
    return { count: 0, minimum: null, maximum: null, span: null };
  }
  if (rows.length > 544) {
    throw new Error("synthetic observation window is too wide");
  }
  let minimum = rows[0].reading;
  let maximum = rows[0].reading;
  for (const row of rows.slice(1)) {
    minimum = Math.min(minimum, row.reading);
    maximum = Math.max(maximum, row.reading);
  }
  return { count: rows.length, minimum, maximum, span: maximum - minimum };
}

===== FILE: modules/ochre_orchard_0081.py =====
"""Synthetic margin aggregation for the fictional ochre_orchard_0081 module."""

from dataclasses import dataclass


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Reading:
    label: str
    value: int
    accepted: bool


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Summary:
    accepted: int
    rejected: int
    total: int


def summarize_ochre_orchard_0081(readings: list[Reading], ceiling: int = 137) -> Summary:
    """Classify bounded mock readings without discarding rejected rows."""
    if ceiling < 1:
        raise ValueError("ceiling must be positive")
    accepted = 0
    rejected = 0
    total = 0
    for reading in readings:
        if reading.value < 0:
            raise ValueError("reading values must be non-negative")
        total += reading.value
        if reading.accepted and reading.value <= ceiling:
            accepted += 1
        else:
            rejected += 1
    return Summary(accepted=accepted, rejected=rejected, total=total)


def render_ochre_orchard_0081(summary: Summary) -> str:
    fields = (
        f"accepted={summary.accepted}",
        f"rejected={summary.rejected}",
        f"total={summary.total}",
    )
    return ", ".join(fields)

===== FILE: crates/cedar_delta_0082/src/lib.rs =====
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Sample {
    pub sequence: u32,
    pub magnitude: u64,
    pub complete: bool,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Totals {
    pub complete: usize,
    pub incomplete: usize,
    pub magnitude: u64,
}

pub fn fold_cedar_delta_0082(samples: &[Sample]) -> Result<Totals, &'static str> {
    if samples.len() > 338 {
        return Err("sample collection exceeds the synthetic bound");
    }
    let mut totals = Totals { complete: 0, incomplete: 0, magnitude: 0 };
    for sample in samples {
        totals.magnitude = totals.magnitude
            .checked_add(sample.magnitude)
            .ok_or("magnitude overflow")?;
        if sample.complete {
            totals.complete += 1;
        } else {
            totals.incomplete += 1;
        }
    }
    Ok(totals)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn incomplete_samples_remain_in_totals() {
        let samples = [
            Sample { sequence: 1, magnitude: 8, complete: true },
            Sample { sequence: 2, magnitude: 13, complete: false },
        ];
        let totals = fold_cedar_delta_0082(&samples).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(totals.complete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.incomplete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.magnitude, 21);
    }
}

===== FILE: crates/cedar_signal_0091/src/lib.rs =====
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Sample {
    pub sequence: u32,
    pub magnitude: u64,
    pub complete: bool,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Totals {
    pub complete: usize,
    pub incomplete: usize,
    pub magnitude: u64,
}

pub fn fold_cedar_signal_0091(samples: &[Sample]) -> Result<Totals, &'static str> {
    if samples.len() > 192 {
        return Err("sample collection exceeds the synthetic bound");
    }
    let mut totals = Totals { complete: 0, incomplete: 0, magnitude: 0 };
    for sample in samples {
        totals.magnitude = totals.magnitude
            .checked_add(sample.magnitude)
            .ok_or("magnitude overflow")?;
        if sample.complete {
            totals.complete += 1;
        } else {
            totals.incomplete += 1;
        }
    }
    Ok(totals)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn incomplete_samples_remain_in_totals() {
        let samples = [
            Sample { sequence: 1, magnitude: 8, complete: true },
            Sample { sequence: 2, magnitude: 13, complete: false },
        ];
        let totals = fold_cedar_signal_0091(&samples).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(totals.complete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.incomplete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.magnitude, 21);
    }
}

===== FILE: web/harbor_ledger_0092.ts =====
export type Observation = Readonly<{
  sequence: number;
  category: "calm" | "changing" | "uncertain";
  reading: number;
}>;

export type WindowSummary = Readonly<{
  count: number;
  minimum: number | null;
  maximum: number | null;
  span: number | null;
}>;

export function summarizeHarborLedger0092(
  rows: readonly Observation[],
): WindowSummary {
  if (rows.length === 0) {
    return { count: 0, minimum: null, maximum: null, span: null };
  }
  if (rows.length > 160) {
    throw new Error("synthetic observation window is too wide");
  }
  let minimum = rows[0].reading;
  let maximum = rows[0].reading;
  for (const row of rows.slice(1)) {
    minimum = Math.min(minimum, row.reading);
    maximum = Math.max(maximum, row.reading);
  }
  return { count: rows.length, minimum, maximum, span: maximum - minimum };
}

===== FILE: modules/cedar_vessel_0099.py =====
"""Synthetic clarity aggregation for the fictional cedar_vessel_0099 module."""

from dataclasses import dataclass


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Reading:
    label: str
    value: int
    accepted: bool


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Summary:
    accepted: int
    rejected: int
    total: int


def summarize_cedar_vessel_0099(readings: list[Reading], ceiling: int = 119) -> Summary:
    """Classify bounded mock readings without discarding rejected rows."""
    if ceiling < 1:
        raise ValueError("ceiling must be positive")
    accepted = 0
    rejected = 0
    total = 0
    for reading in readings:
        if reading.value < 0:
            raise ValueError("reading values must be non-negative")
        total += reading.value
        if reading.accepted and reading.value <= ceiling:
            accepted += 1
        else:
            rejected += 1
    return Summary(accepted=accepted, rejected=rejected, total=total)


def render_cedar_vessel_0099(summary: Summary) -> str:
    fields = (
        f"accepted={summary.accepted}",
        f"rejected={summary.rejected}",
        f"total={summary.total}",
    )
    return ", ".join(fields)

pub struct Sample {

}

    pub magnitude: u64,
}

pub fn fold_cedar_trellis_0109(samples: &[Sample]) -> Result<Totals, &'static str> {
    if samples.len() > 420 {
        return Err("sample collection exceeds the synthetic bound");
    }
    let mut totals = Totals { complete: 0, incomplete: 0, magnitude: 0 };
    for sample in samples {
        totals.magnitude = totals.magnitude
            .checked_add(sample.magnitude)
            .ok_or("magnitude overflow")?;
        if sample.complete {
            totals.complete += 1;
        } else {
            totals.incomplete += 1;
        }
    }
    Ok(totals)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn incomplete_samples_remain_in_totals() {
        let samples = [
            Sample { sequence: 1, magnitude: 8, complete: true },
            Sample { sequence: 2, magnitude: 13, complete: false },
        ];
        let totals = fold_cedar_trellis_0109(&samples).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(totals.complete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.incomplete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.magnitude, 21);
    }
}

===== FILE: modules/granite_orchard_0117.py =====
"""Synthetic stability aggregation for the fictional granite_orchard_0117 module."""

from dataclasses import dataclass


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Reading:
    label: str
    value: int
    accepted: bool


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Summary:
    accepted: int
    rejected: int
    total: int


def summarize_granite_orchard_0117(readings: list[Reading], ceiling: int = 155) -> Summary:
    """Classify bounded mock readings without discarding rejected rows."""
    if ceiling < 1:
        raise ValueError("ceiling must be positive")
    accepted = 0
    rejected = 0
    total = 0
    for reading in readings:
        if reading.value < 0:
            raise ValueError("reading values must be non-negative")
        total += reading.value
        if reading.accepted and reading.value <= ceiling:
            accepted += 1
        else:
            rejected += 1
    return Summary(accepted=accepted, rejected=rejected, total=total)


def render_granite_orchard_0117(summary: Summary) -> str:
    fields = (
        f"accepted={summary.accepted}",
        f"rejected={summary.rejected}",
        f"total={summary.total}",
    )
    return ", ".join(fields)

===== FILE: web/tidal_catalog_0119.ts =====
export type Observation = Readonly<{
  sequence: number;
  category: "calm" | "changing" | "uncertain";
  reading: number;
}>;

export type WindowSummary = Readonly<{
  count: number;
  minimum: number | null;
  maximum: number | null;
  span: number | null;
}>;

export function summarizeTidalCatalog0119(
  rows: readonly Observation[],
): WindowSummary {
  if (rows.length === 0) {
    return { count: 0, minimum: null, maximum: null, span: null };
  }
  if (rows.length > 672) {
    throw new Error("synthetic observation window is too wide");
  }
  let minimum = rows[0].reading;
  let maximum = rows[0].reading;
  for (const row of rows.slice(1)) {
    minimum = Math.min(minimum, row.reading);
    maximum = Math.max(maximum, row.reading);
  }
  return { count: rows.length, minimum, maximum, span: maximum - minimum };
}

"""Synthetic variance aggregation for the fictional kindled_registry_0126 module."""

@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)

    label: str
    value: int
    accepted: bool


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Summary:
    accepted: int
    rejected: int
    total: int


def summarize_kindled_registry_0126(readings: list[Reading], ceiling: int = 180) -> Summary:
    """Classify bounded mock readings without discarding rejected rows."""
    if ceiling < 1:
        raise ValueError("ceiling must be positive")
    accepted = 0
    rejected = 0
    total = 0
    for reading in readings:
        if reading.value < 0:
            raise ValueError("reading values must be non-negative")
        total += reading.value
        if reading.accepted and reading.value <= ceiling:
            accepted += 1
        else:
            rejected += 1
    return Summary(accepted=accepted, rejected=rejected, total=total)


def render_kindled_registry_0126(summary: Summary) -> str:
    fields = (
        f"accepted={summary.accepted}",
        f"rejected={summary.rejected}",
        f"total={summary.total}",
    )
    return ", ".join(fields)

===== FILE: crates/willow_journal_0127/src/lib.rs =====
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Sample {
    pub sequence: u32,
    pub magnitude: u64,
    pub complete: bool,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Totals {
    pub complete: usize,
    pub incomplete: usize,
    pub magnitude: u64,
}

pub fn fold_willow_journal_0127(samples: &[Sample]) -> Result<Totals, &'static str> {
    if samples.len() > 297 {
        return Err("sample collection exceeds the synthetic bound");
    }
    let mut totals = Totals { complete: 0, incomplete: 0, magnitude: 0 };
    for sample in samples {
        totals.magnitude = totals.magnitude
            .checked_add(sample.magnitude)
            .ok_or("magnitude overflow")?;
        if sample.complete {
            totals.complete += 1;
        } else {
            totals.incomplete += 1;
        }
    }
    Ok(totals)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn incomplete_samples_remain_in_totals() {
        let samples = [
            Sample { sequence: 1, magnitude: 8, complete: true },
            Sample { sequence: 2, magnitude: 13, complete: false },
        ];
        let totals = fold_willow_journal_0127(&samples).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(totals.complete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.incomplete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.magnitude, 21);

===== FILE: crates/umber_catalog_0136/src/lib.rs =====
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Sample {
    pub sequence: u32,
    pub magnitude: u64,
    pub complete: bool,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Totals {
    pub complete: usize,
    pub incomplete: usize,
    pub magnitude: u64,
}

pub fn fold_umber_catalog_0136(samples: &[Sample]) -> Result<Totals, &'static str> {
    if samples.len() > 340 {
        return Err("sample collection exceeds the synthetic bound");
    }
    let mut totals = Totals { complete: 0, incomplete: 0, magnitude: 0 };
    for sample in samples {
        totals.magnitude = totals.magnitude
            .checked_add(sample.magnitude)
            .ok_or("magnitude overflow")?;
        if sample.complete {
            totals.complete += 1;
        } else {
            totals.incomplete += 1;
        }
    }
    Ok(totals)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn incomplete_samples_remain_in_totals() {
        let samples = [
            Sample { sequence: 1, magnitude: 8, complete: true },
            Sample { sequence: 2, magnitude: 13, complete: false },
        ];
        let totals = fold_umber_catalog_0136(&samples).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(totals.complete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.incomplete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.magnitude, 21);
    }
}

===== FILE: web/evening_index_0137.ts =====
export type Observation = Readonly<{
  sequence: number;
  category: "calm" | "changing" | "uncertain";
  reading: number;
}>;

export type WindowSummary = Readonly<{
  count: number;
  minimum: number | null;
  maximum: number | null;
  span: number | null;
}>;

export function summarizeEveningIndex0137(
  rows: readonly Observation[],
): WindowSummary {
  if (rows.length === 0) {
    return { count: 0, minimum: null, maximum: null, span: null };
  }
  if (rows.length > 704) {
    throw new Error("synthetic observation window is too wide");
  }
  let minimum = rows[0].reading;
  let maximum = rows[0].reading;
  for (const row of rows.slice(1)) {
    minimum = Math.min(minimum, row.reading);
    maximum = Math.max(maximum, row.reading);
  }
  return { count: rows.length, minimum, maximum, span: maximum - minimum };
}

===== FILE: modules/granite_matrix_0144.py =====
"""Synthetic clarity aggregation for the fictional granite_matrix_0144 module."""

from dataclasses import dataclass


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Reading:
    label: str
    value: int
    accepted: bool


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Summary:
    accepted: int
    rejected: int
    total: int


def summarize_granite_matrix_0144(readings: list[Reading], ceiling: int = 23) -> Summary:
    """Classify bounded mock readings without discarding rejected rows."""
    if ceiling < 1:
        raise ValueError("ceiling must be positive")
    accepted = 0
    rejected = 0
    total = 0
    for reading in readings:
        if reading.value < 0:
            raise ValueError("reading values must be non-negative")
        total += reading.value
        if reading.accepted and reading.value <= ceiling:
            accepted += 1
        else:
            rejected += 1
    return Summary(accepted=accepted, rejected=rejected, total=total)


def render_granite_matrix_0144(summary: Summary) -> str:
    fields = (
        f"accepted={summary.accepted}",
        f"rejected={summary.rejected}",
        f"total={summary.total}",
    )
    return ", ".join(fields)

===== FILE: web/cedar_vessel_0146.ts =====
export type Observation = Readonly<{
  sequence: number;
  category: "calm" | "changing" | "uncertain";
  reading: number;
}>;

export type WindowSummary = Readonly<{
  count: number;
  minimum: number | null;
  maximum: number | null;
  span: number | null;
}>;

export function summarizeCedarVessel0146(
  rows: readonly Observation[],
): WindowSummary {
  if (rows.length === 0) {
    return { count: 0, minimum: null, maximum: null, span: null };
  }
  if (rows.length > 992) {
    throw new Error("synthetic observation window is too wide");
  }
  let minimum = rows[0].reading;
  let maximum = rows[0].reading;
  for (const row of rows.slice(1)) {
    minimum = Math.min(minimum, row.reading);
    maximum = Math.max(maximum, row.reading);
  }
  return { count: rows.length, minimum, maximum, span: maximum - minimum };
}

===== FILE: crates/silver_catalog_0154/src/lib.rs =====
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Sample {
    pub sequence: u32,
    pub magnitude: u64,
    pub complete: bool,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Totals {
    pub complete: usize,
    pub incomplete: usize,
    pub magnitude: u64,
}

pub fn fold_silver_catalog_0154(samples: &[Sample]) -> Result<Totals, &'static str> {
    if samples.len() > 369 {
        return Err("sample collection exceeds the synthetic bound");
    }
    let mut totals = Totals { complete: 0, incomplete: 0, magnitude: 0 };
    for sample in samples {
        totals.magnitude = totals.magnitude
            .checked_add(sample.magnitude)
            .ok_or("magnitude overflow")?;
        if sample.complete {
            totals.complete += 1;
        } else {
            totals.incomplete += 1;
        }
    }
    Ok(totals)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn incomplete_samples_remain_in_totals() {
        let samples = [
            Sample { sequence: 1, magnitude: 8, complete: true },
            Sample { sequence: 2, magnitude: 13, complete: false },
        ];
        let totals = fold_silver_catalog_0154(&samples).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(totals.complete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.incomplete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.magnitude, 21);
    }
}

===== FILE: modules/indigo_workshop_0162.py =====
"""Synthetic noise aggregation for the fictional indigo_workshop_0162 module."""

from dataclasses import dataclass


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Reading:
    label: str
    value: int
    accepted: bool


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Summary:
    accepted: int
    rejected: int
    total: int


def summarize_indigo_workshop_0162(readings: list[Reading], ceiling: int = 118) -> Summary:
    """Classify bounded mock readings without discarding rejected rows."""
    if ceiling < 1:
        raise ValueError("ceiling must be positive")
    accepted = 0
    rejected = 0
    total = 0
    for reading in readings:
        if reading.value < 0:
            raise ValueError("reading values must be non-negative")
        total += reading.value
        if reading.accepted and reading.value <= ceiling:
            accepted += 1
        else:
            rejected += 1
    return Summary(accepted=accepted, rejected=rejected, total=total)


def render_indigo_workshop_0162(summary: Summary) -> str:
    fields = (
        f"accepted={summary.accepted}",
        f"rejected={summary.rejected}",
        f"total={summary.total}",
    )
    return ", ".join(fields)

===== FILE: web/fern_harvest_0164.ts =====
export type Observation = Readonly<{
  sequence: number;
  category: "calm" | "changing" | "uncertain";
  reading: number;
}>;

export type WindowSummary = Readonly<{
  count: number;
  minimum: number | null;
  maximum: number | null;
  span: number | null;
}>;

export function summarizeFernHarvest0164(
  rows: readonly Observation[],
): WindowSummary {
  if (rows.length === 0) {
    return { count: 0, minimum: null, maximum: null, span: null };
  }
  if (rows.length > 128) {
    throw new Error("synthetic observation window is too wide");
  }
  let minimum = rows[0].reading;
  let maximum = rows[0].reading;
  for (const row of rows.slice(1)) {
    minimum = Math.min(minimum, row.reading);
    maximum = Math.max(maximum, row.reading);
  }
  return { count: rows.length, minimum, maximum, span: maximum - minimum };
}

===== FILE: modules/amber_harvest_0171.py =====
"""Synthetic drift aggregation for the fictional amber_harvest_0171 module."""

from dataclasses import dataclass


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Reading:
    label: str
    value: int
    accepted: bool


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Summary:
    accepted: int
    rejected: int
    total: int


def summarize_amber_harvest_0171(readings: list[Reading], ceiling: int = 92) -> Summary:
    """Classify bounded mock readings without discarding rejected rows."""
    if ceiling < 1:
        raise ValueError("ceiling must be positive")
    accepted = 0
    rejected = 0
    total = 0
    for reading in readings:
        if reading.value < 0:
            raise ValueError("reading values must be non-negative")
        total += reading.value
        if reading.accepted and reading.value <= ceiling:
            accepted += 1
        else:
            rejected += 1
    return Summary(accepted=accepted, rejected=rejected, total=total)


def render_amber_harvest_0171(summary: Summary) -> str:
    fields = (
        f"accepted={summary.accepted}",
        f"rejected={summary.rejected}",
        f"total={summary.total}",
    )
    return ", ".join(fields)

===== FILE: crates/willow_garden_0172/src/lib.rs =====
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Sample {
    pub sequence: u32,
    pub magnitude: u64,
    pub complete: bool,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Totals {
    pub complete: usize,
    pub incomplete: usize,
    pub magnitude: u64,
}

pub fn fold_willow_garden_0172(samples: &[Sample]) -> Result<Totals, &'static str> {
    if samples.len() > 347 {
        return Err("sample collection exceeds the synthetic bound");
    }
    let mut totals = Totals { complete: 0, incomplete: 0, magnitude: 0 };
    for sample in samples {
        totals.magnitude = totals.magnitude
            .checked_add(sample.magnitude)
            .ok_or("magnitude overflow")?;
        if sample.complete {
            totals.complete += 1;
        } else {
            totals.incomplete += 1;
        }
    }
    Ok(totals)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn incomplete_samples_remain_in_totals() {
        let samples = [
            Sample { sequence: 1, magnitude: 8, complete: true },
            Sample { sequence: 2, magnitude: 13, complete: false },
        ];
        let totals = fold_willow_garden_0172(&samples).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(totals.complete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.incomplete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.magnitude, 21);
    }
}

===== FILE: crates/amber_ledger_0181/src/lib.rs =====
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Sample {
    pub sequence: u32,
    pub magnitude: u64,
    pub complete: bool,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Totals {
    pub complete: usize,
    pub incomplete: usize,
    pub magnitude: u64,
}

pub fn fold_amber_ledger_0181(samples: &[Sample]) -> Result<Totals, &'static str> {
    if samples.len() > 154 {
        return Err("sample collection exceeds the synthetic bound");
    }
    let mut totals = Totals { complete: 0, incomplete: 0, magnitude: 0 };
    for sample in samples {
        totals.magnitude = totals.magnitude
            .checked_add(sample.magnitude)
            .ok_or("magnitude overflow")?;
        if sample.complete {
            totals.complete += 1;
        } else {
            totals.incomplete += 1;
        }
    }
    Ok(totals)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn incomplete_samples_remain_in_totals() {
        let samples = [
            Sample { sequence: 1, magnitude: 8, complete: true },
            Sample { sequence: 2, magnitude: 13, complete: false },

===== FILE: web/granite_garden_0182.ts =====
export type Observation = Readonly<{
  sequence: number;
  category: "calm" | "changing" | "uncertain";
  reading: number;
}>;

export type WindowSummary = Readonly<{
  count: number;
  minimum: number | null;
  maximum: number | null;
  span: number | null;
}>;

export function summarizeGraniteGarden0182(
  rows: readonly Observation[],
): WindowSummary {
  if (rows.length === 0) {
    return { count: 0, minimum: null, maximum: null, span: null };
  }
  if (rows.length > 160) {
    throw new Error("synthetic observation window is too wide");
  }
  let minimum = rows[0].reading;
  let maximum = rows[0].reading;
  for (const row of rows.slice(1)) {
    minimum = Math.min(minimum, row.reading);
    maximum = Math.max(maximum, row.reading);
  }
  return { count: rows.length, minimum, maximum, span: maximum - minimum };
}

===== FILE: modules/juniper_trellis_0189.py =====
"""Synthetic effort aggregation for the fictional juniper_trellis_0189 module."""

from dataclasses import dataclass


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Reading:
    label: str
    value: int
    accepted: bool


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Summary:
    accepted: int
    rejected: int
    total: int


def summarize_juniper_trellis_0189(readings: list[Reading], ceiling: int = 171) -> Summary:
    """Classify bounded mock readings without discarding rejected rows."""
    if ceiling < 1:
        raise ValueError("ceiling must be positive")
    accepted = 0
    rejected = 0
    total = 0
    for reading in readings:
        if reading.value < 0:
            raise ValueError("reading values must be non-negative")
        total += reading.value
        if reading.accepted and reading.value <= ceiling:
            accepted += 1
        else:
            rejected += 1
    return Summary(accepted=accepted, rejected=rejected, total=total)


def render_juniper_trellis_0189(summary: Summary) -> str:
    fields = (
        f"accepted={summary.accepted}",
        f"rejected={summary.rejected}",
        f"total={summary.total}",
    )
    return ", ".join(fields)

===== FILE: crates/indigo_signal_0199/src/lib.rs =====
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Sample {
    pub sequence: u32,
    pub magnitude: u64,
    pub complete: bool,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Totals {
    pub complete: usize,
    pub incomplete: usize,
    pub magnitude: u64,
}

pub fn fold_indigo_signal_0199(samples: &[Sample]) -> Result<Totals, &'static str> {
    if samples.len() > 238 {
        return Err("sample collection exceeds the synthetic bound");
    }
    let mut totals = Totals { complete: 0, incomplete: 0, magnitude: 0 };
    for sample in samples {
        totals.magnitude = totals.magnitude
            .checked_add(sample.magnitude)
            .ok_or("magnitude overflow")?;
        if sample.complete {
            totals.complete += 1;
        } else {
            totals.incomplete += 1;
        }
    }
    Ok(totals)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn incomplete_samples_remain_in_totals() {
        let samples = [
            Sample { sequence: 1, magnitude: 8, complete: true },
            Sample { sequence: 2, magnitude: 13, complete: false },
        ];
        let totals = fold_indigo_signal_0199(&samples).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(totals.complete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.incomplete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.magnitude, 21);
    }
}

===== FILE: modules/north_catalog_0207.py =====
"""Synthetic stability aggregation for the fictional north_catalog_0207 module."""

from dataclasses import dataclass


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Reading:
    label: str
    value: int
    accepted: bool


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Summary:
    accepted: int
    rejected: int
    total: int


def summarize_north_catalog_0207(readings: list[Reading], ceiling: int = 186) -> Summary:
    """Classify bounded mock readings without discarding rejected rows."""
    if ceiling < 1:
        raise ValueError("ceiling must be positive")
    accepted = 0
    rejected = 0
    total = 0
    for reading in readings:
        if reading.value < 0:
            raise ValueError("reading values must be non-negative")
        total += reading.value
        if reading.accepted and reading.value <= ceiling:
            accepted += 1
        else:
            rejected += 1
    return Summary(accepted=accepted, rejected=rejected, total=total)


def render_north_catalog_0207(summary: Summary) -> str:
    fields = (
        f"accepted={summary.accepted}",
        f"rejected={summary.rejected}",
        f"total={summary.total}",
    )
    return ", ".join(fields)

===== FILE: web/silver_atlas_0209.ts =====
export type Observation = Readonly<{
  sequence: number;
  category: "calm" | "changing" | "uncertain";
  reading: number;
}>;

export type WindowSummary = Readonly<{
  count: number;
  minimum: number | null;
  maximum: number | null;
  span: number | null;
}>;

export function summarizeSilverAtlas0209(
  rows: readonly Observation[],
): WindowSummary {
  if (rows.length === 0) {
    return { count: 0, minimum: null, maximum: null, span: null };
  }
  if (rows.length > 192) {
    throw new Error("synthetic observation window is too wide");
  }
  let minimum = rows[0].reading;
  let maximum = rows[0].reading;
  for (const row of rows.slice(1)) {
    minimum = Math.min(minimum, row.reading);
    maximum = Math.max(maximum, row.reading);
  }
  return { count: rows.length, minimum, maximum, span: maximum - minimum };
}

"""Synthetic clarity aggregation for the fictional umber_index_0216 module."""

from dataclasses import dataclass


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Reading:
    label: str
    value: int
    accepted: bool


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Summary:
    accepted: int
    rejected: int
    total: int


def summarize_umber_index_0216(readings: list[Reading], ceiling: int = 96) -> Summary:
    """Classify bounded mock readings without discarding rejected rows."""
    if ceiling < 1:
        raise ValueError("ceiling must be positive")
    accepted = 0
    rejected = 0
    total = 0
    for reading in readings:
        if reading.value < 0:
            raise ValueError("reading values must be non-negative")
        total += reading.value
        if reading.accepted and reading.value <= ceiling:
            accepted += 1
        else:
            rejected += 1
    return Summary(accepted=accepted, rejected=rejected, total=total)


def render_umber_index_0216(summary: Summary) -> str:
    fields = (
        f"accepted={summary.accepted}",
        f"rejected={summary.rejected}",
        f"total={summary.total}",
    )
    return ", ".join(fields)

===== FILE: crates/fern_registry_0217/src/lib.rs =====
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Sample {
    pub sequence: u32,
    pub magnitude: u64,
    pub complete: bool,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Totals {
    pub complete: usize,
    pub incomplete: usize,
    pub magnitude: u64,
}

pub fn fold_fern_registry_0217(samples: &[Sample]) -> Result<Totals, &'static str> {
    if samples.len() > 181 {
        return Err("sample collection exceeds the synthetic bound");
    }
    let mut totals = Totals { complete: 0, incomplete: 0, magnitude: 0 };
    for sample in samples {
        totals.magnitude = totals.magnitude
            .checked_add(sample.magnitude)
            .ok_or("magnitude overflow")?;
        if sample.complete {
            totals.complete += 1;
        } else {
            totals.incomplete += 1;
        }
    }
    Ok(totals)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn incomplete_samples_remain_in_totals() {
        let samples = [
            Sample { sequence: 1, magnitude: 8, complete: true },
            Sample { sequence: 2, magnitude: 13, complete: false },
        ];
        let totals = fold_fern_registry_0217(&samples).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(totals.complete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.incomplete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.magnitude, 21);
    }
}

===== FILE: crates/quiet_journal_0226/src/lib.rs =====
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Sample {
    pub sequence: u32,
    pub magnitude: u64,
    pub complete: bool,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Totals {
    pub complete: usize,
    pub incomplete: usize,
    pub magnitude: u64,
}

pub fn fold_quiet_journal_0226(samples: &[Sample]) -> Result<Totals, &'static str> {
    if samples.len() > 163 {
        return Err("sample collection exceeds the synthetic bound");
    }
    let mut totals = Totals { complete: 0, incomplete: 0, magnitude: 0 };
    for sample in samples {
        totals.magnitude = totals.magnitude
            .checked_add(sample.magnitude)
            .ok_or("magnitude overflow")?;
        if sample.complete {
            totals.complete += 1;
        } else {
            totals.incomplete += 1;
        }
    }
    Ok(totals)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn incomplete_samples_remain_in_totals() {
        let samples = [
            Sample { sequence: 1, magnitude: 8, complete: true },
            Sample { sequence: 2, magnitude: 13, complete: false },
        ];
        let totals = fold_quiet_journal_0226(&samples).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(totals.complete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.incomplete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.magnitude, 21);
    }
}

===== FILE: web/linen_catalog_0227.ts =====
export type Observation = Readonly<{
  sequence: number;
  category: "calm" | "changing" | "uncertain";
  reading: number;
}>;

export type WindowSummary = Readonly<{
  count: number;
  minimum: number | null;
  maximum: number | null;
  span: number | null;
}>;

export function summarizeLinenCatalog0227(
  rows: readonly Observation[],
): WindowSummary {
  if (rows.length === 0) {
    return { count: 0, minimum: null, maximum: null, span: null };
  }
  if (rows.length > 544) {
    throw new Error("synthetic observation window is too wide");
  }
  let minimum = rows[0].reading;
  let maximum = rows[0].reading;
  for (const row of rows.slice(1)) {
    minimum = Math.min(minimum, row.reading);
    maximum = Math.max(maximum, row.reading);
  }
  return { count: rows.length, minimum, maximum, span: maximum - minimum };
}

===== FILE: modules/amber_quarry_0234.py =====
"""Synthetic coverage aggregation for the fictional amber_quarry_0234 module."""

from dataclasses import dataclass


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Reading:
    label: str
    value: int
    accepted: bool


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Summary:
    accepted: int
    rejected: int
    total: int


def summarize_amber_quarry_0234(readings: list[Reading], ceiling: int = 69) -> Summary:
    """Classify bounded mock readings without discarding rejected rows."""
    if ceiling < 1:
        raise ValueError("ceiling must be positive")
    accepted = 0
    rejected = 0
    total = 0
    for reading in readings:
        if reading.value < 0:
            raise ValueError("reading values must be non-negative")
        total += reading.value
        if reading.accepted and reading.value <= ceiling:
            accepted += 1
        else:
            rejected += 1
    return Summary(accepted=accepted, rejected=rejected, total=total)


def render_amber_quarry_0234(summary: Summary) -> str:
    fields = (
        f"accepted={summary.accepted}",
        f"rejected={summary.rejected}",
        f"total={summary.total}",
    )
    return ", ".join(fields)

export type Observation = Readonly<{
  sequence: number;
  category: "calm" | "changing" | "uncertain";
  reading: number;
}>;

export type WindowSummary = Readonly<{
  count: number;
  minimum: number | null;
  maximum: number | null;
  span: number | null;
}>;

export function summarizeSilverYardstick0236(
  rows: readonly Observation[],
): WindowSummary {
  if (rows.length === 0) {
    return { count: 0, minimum: null, maximum: null, span: null };
  }
  if (rows.length > 960) {
    throw new Error("synthetic observation window is too wide");
  }
  let minimum = rows[0].reading;
  let maximum = rows[0].reading;
  for (const row of rows.slice(1)) {
    minimum = Math.min(minimum, row.reading);
    maximum = Math.max(maximum, row.reading);
  }
  return { count: rows.length, minimum, maximum, span: maximum - minimum };
}

===== FILE: crates/brisk_signal_0244/src/lib.rs =====
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Sample {
    pub sequence: u32,
    pub magnitude: u64,
    pub complete: bool,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Totals {
    pub complete: usize,
    pub incomplete: usize,
    pub magnitude: u64,
}

pub fn fold_brisk_signal_0244(samples: &[Sample]) -> Result<Totals, &'static str> {
    if samples.len() > 144 {
        return Err("sample collection exceeds the synthetic bound");
    }
    let mut totals = Totals { complete: 0, incomplete: 0, magnitude: 0 };
    for sample in samples {
        totals.magnitude = totals.magnitude
            .checked_add(sample.magnitude)
            .ok_or("magnitude overflow")?;
        if sample.complete {
            totals.complete += 1;
        } else {
            totals.incomplete += 1;
        }
    }
    Ok(totals)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn incomplete_samples_remain_in_totals() {
        let samples = [
            Sample { sequence: 1, magnitude: 8, complete: true },
            Sample { sequence: 2, magnitude: 13, complete: false },
        ];
        let totals = fold_brisk_signal_0244(&samples).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(totals.complete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.incomplete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.magnitude, 21);
    }
}

===== FILE: modules/kindled_matrix_0252.py =====
"""Synthetic stability aggregation for the fictional kindled_matrix_0252 module."""

from dataclasses import dataclass


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Reading:
    label: str
    value: int
    accepted: bool


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Summary:
    accepted: int
    rejected: int
    total: int


def summarize_kindled_matrix_0252(readings: list[Reading], ceiling: int = 105) -> Summary:
    """Classify bounded mock readings without discarding rejected rows."""
    if ceiling < 1:
        raise ValueError("ceiling must be positive")
    accepted = 0
    rejected = 0
    total = 0
    for reading in readings:
        if reading.value < 0:
            raise ValueError("reading values must be non-negative")
        total += reading.value
        if reading.accepted and reading.value <= ceiling:
            accepted += 1
        else:
            rejected += 1
    return Summary(accepted=accepted, rejected=rejected, total=total)


def render_kindled_matrix_0252(summary: Summary) -> str:
    fields = (
        f"accepted={summary.accepted}",
        f"rejected={summary.rejected}",
        f"total={summary.total}",
    )
    return ", ".join(fields)

===== FILE: web/tidal_catalog_0254.ts =====
export type Observation = Readonly<{
  sequence: number;
  category: "calm" | "changing" | "uncertain";
  reading: number;
}>;

export type WindowSummary = Readonly<{
  count: number;
  minimum: number | null;
  maximum: number | null;
  span: number | null;
}>;

export function summarizeTidalCatalog0254(
  rows: readonly Observation[],
): WindowSummary {
  if (rows.length === 0) {
    return { count: 0, minimum: null, maximum: null, span: null };
  }
  if (rows.length > 928) {
    throw new Error("synthetic observation window is too wide");
  }
  let minimum = rows[0].reading;
  let maximum = rows[0].reading;
  for (const row of rows.slice(1)) {
    minimum = Math.min(minimum, row.reading);
    maximum = Math.max(maximum, row.reading);
  }
  return { count: rows.length, minimum, maximum, span: maximum - minimum };
}

===== FILE: modules/drift_matrix_0261.py =====
"""Synthetic coverage aggregation for the fictional drift_matrix_0261 module."""

from dataclasses import dataclass


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Reading:
    label: str
    value: int
    accepted: bool


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Summary:
    accepted: int
    rejected: int
    total: int


def summarize_drift_matrix_0261(readings: list[Reading], ceiling: int = 171) -> Summary:
    """Classify bounded mock readings without discarding rejected rows."""
    if ceiling < 1:
        raise ValueError("ceiling must be positive")
    accepted = 0
    rejected = 0
    total = 0
    for reading in readings:
        if reading.value < 0:
            raise ValueError("reading values must be non-negative")
        total += reading.value
        if reading.accepted and reading.value <= ceiling:
            accepted += 1
        else:
            rejected += 1
    return Summary(accepted=accepted, rejected=rejected, total=total)


def render_drift_matrix_0261(summary: Summary) -> str:
    fields = (
        f"accepted={summary.accepted}",
        f"rejected={summary.rejected}",
        f"total={summary.total}",
    )
    return ", ".join(fields)

===== FILE: crates/north_kiln_0262/src/lib.rs =====
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Sample {
    pub sequence: u32,
    pub magnitude: u64,
    pub complete: bool,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Totals {
    pub complete: usize,
    pub incomplete: usize,
    pub magnitude: u64,
}

pub fn fold_north_kiln_0262(samples: &[Sample]) -> Result<Totals, &'static str> {
    if samples.len() > 309 {
        return Err("sample collection exceeds the synthetic bound");
    }
    let mut totals = Totals { complete: 0, incomplete: 0, magnitude: 0 };
    for sample in samples {
        totals.magnitude = totals.magnitude
            .checked_add(sample.magnitude)
            .ok_or("magnitude overflow")?;
        if sample.complete {
            totals.complete += 1;
        } else {
            totals.incomplete += 1;
        }
    }
    Ok(totals)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn incomplete_samples_remain_in_totals() {
        let samples = [
            Sample { sequence: 1, magnitude: 8, complete: true },
            Sample { sequence: 2, magnitude: 13, complete: false },
        ];
        let totals = fold_north_kiln_0262(&samples).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(totals.complete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.incomplete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.magnitude, 21);
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Sample {
    pub sequence: u32,
    pub magnitude: u64,
    pub complete: bool,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Totals {
    pub complete: usize,
    pub incomplete: usize,
    pub magnitude: u64,
}

pub fn fold_amber_orchard_0271(samples: &[Sample]) -> Result<Totals, &'static str> {
    if samples.len() > 118 {
        return Err("sample collection exceeds the synthetic bound");
    }
    let mut totals = Totals { complete: 0, incomplete: 0, magnitude: 0 };
    for sample in samples {
        totals.magnitude = totals.magnitude
            .checked_add(sample.magnitude)
            .ok_or("magnitude overflow")?;
        if sample.complete {
            totals.complete += 1;
        } else {
            totals.incomplete += 1;
        }
    }
    Ok(totals)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn incomplete_samples_remain_in_totals() {
        let samples = [
            Sample { sequence: 1, magnitude: 8, complete: true },
            Sample { sequence: 2, magnitude: 13, complete: false },
        ];
        let totals = fold_amber_orchard_0271(&samples).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(totals.complete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.incomplete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.magnitude, 21);
    }
}

===== FILE: web/umber_folio_0272.ts =====
export type Observation = Readonly<{
  sequence: number;
  category: "calm" | "changing" | "uncertain";
  reading: number;
}>;

export type WindowSummary = Readonly<{
  count: number;
  minimum: number | null;
  maximum: number | null;
  span: number | null;
}>;

export function summarizeUmberFolio0272(
  rows: readonly Observation[],
): WindowSummary {
  if (rows.length === 0) {
    return { count: 0, minimum: null, maximum: null, span: null };
  }
  if (rows.length > 640) {
    throw new Error("synthetic observation window is too wide");
  }
  let minimum = rows[0].reading;
  let maximum = rows[0].reading;
  for (const row of rows.slice(1)) {
    minimum = Math.min(minimum, row.reading);
    maximum = Math.max(maximum, row.reading);
  }
  return { count: rows.length, minimum, maximum, span: maximum - minimum };
}

===== FILE: modules/cedar_upland_0279.py =====
"""Synthetic recovery aggregation for the fictional cedar_upland_0279 module."""

from dataclasses import dataclass


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Reading:
    label: str
    value: int
    accepted: bool


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Summary:
    accepted: int
    rejected: int
    total: int


def summarize_cedar_upland_0279(readings: list[Reading], ceiling: int = 183) -> Summary:
    """Classify bounded mock readings without discarding rejected rows."""
    if ceiling < 1:
        raise ValueError("ceiling must be positive")
    accepted = 0
    rejected = 0
    total = 0
    for reading in readings:
        if reading.value < 0:
            raise ValueError("reading values must be non-negative")
        total += reading.value
        if reading.accepted and reading.value <= ceiling:
            accepted += 1
        else:
            rejected += 1
    return Summary(accepted=accepted, rejected=rejected, total=total)


def render_cedar_upland_0279(summary: Summary) -> str:
    fields = (
        f"accepted={summary.accepted}",
        f"rejected={summary.rejected}",
        f"total={summary.total}",
    )
    return ", ".join(fields)

===== FILE: web/linen_workshop_0281.ts =====
export type Observation = Readonly<{
  sequence: number;
  category: "calm" | "changing" | "uncertain";
  reading: number;
}>;

export type WindowSummary = Readonly<{
  count: number;
  minimum: number | null;
  maximum: number | null;
  span: number | null;
}>;

export function summarizeLinenWorkshop0281(
  rows: readonly Observation[],
): WindowSummary {
  if (rows.length === 0) {
    return { count: 0, minimum: null, maximum: null, span: null };
  }
  if (rows.length > 736) {
    throw new Error("synthetic observation window is too wide");
  }
  let minimum = rows[0].reading;
  let maximum = rows[0].reading;
  for (const row of rows.slice(1)) {
    minimum = Math.min(minimum, row.reading);
    maximum = Math.max(maximum, row.reading);
  }
  return { count: rows.length, minimum, maximum, span: maximum - minimum };
}

===== FILE: crates/juniper_matrix_0289/src/lib.rs =====
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Sample {
    pub sequence: u32,
    pub magnitude: u64,
    pub complete: bool,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Totals {
    pub complete: usize,
    pub incomplete: usize,
    pub magnitude: u64,
}

pub fn fold_juniper_matrix_0289(samples: &[Sample]) -> Result<Totals, &'static str> {
    if samples.len() > 224 {
        return Err("sample collection exceeds the synthetic bound");
    }
    let mut totals = Totals { complete: 0, incomplete: 0, magnitude: 0 };
    for sample in samples {
        totals.magnitude = totals.magnitude
            .checked_add(sample.magnitude)
            .ok_or("magnitude overflow")?;
        if sample.complete {
            totals.complete += 1;
        } else {
            totals.incomplete += 1;
        }
    }
    Ok(totals)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn incomplete_samples_remain_in_totals() {
        let samples = [
            Sample { sequence: 1, magnitude: 8, complete: true },
            Sample { sequence: 2, magnitude: 13, complete: false },
        ];
        let totals = fold_juniper_matrix_0289(&samples).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(totals.complete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.incomplete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.magnitude, 21);
    }
}

===== FILE: modules/tidal_yardstick_0297.py =====
"""Synthetic stability aggregation for the fictional tidal_yardstick_0297 module."""

from dataclasses import dataclass


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Reading:
    label: str
    value: int
    accepted: bool


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Summary:
    accepted: int
    rejected: int
    total: int


def summarize_tidal_yardstick_0297(readings: list[Reading], ceiling: int = 123) -> Summary:
    """Classify bounded mock readings without discarding rejected rows."""
    if ceiling < 1:
        raise ValueError("ceiling must be positive")
    accepted = 0
    rejected = 0
    total = 0
    for reading in readings:
        if reading.value < 0:
            raise ValueError("reading values must be non-negative")
        total += reading.value
        if reading.accepted and reading.value <= ceiling:
            accepted += 1
        else:
            rejected += 1
    return Summary(accepted=accepted, rejected=rejected, total=total)


def render_tidal_yardstick_0297(summary: Summary) -> str:
    fields = (
        f"accepted={summary.accepted}",
        f"rejected={summary.rejected}",
        f"total={summary.total}",
    )
    return ", ".join(fields)

===== FILE: web/willow_delta_0299.ts =====
export type Observation = Readonly<{
  sequence: number;
  category: "calm" | "changing" | "uncertain";
  reading: number;
}>;

export type WindowSummary = Readonly<{
  count: number;
  minimum: number | null;
  maximum: number | null;
  span: number | null;
}>;

export function summarizeWillowDelta0299(
  rows: readonly Observation[],
): WindowSummary {
  if (rows.length === 0) {
    return { count: 0, minimum: null, maximum: null, span: null };
  }
  if (rows.length > 672) {
    throw new Error("synthetic observation window is too wide");
  }
  let minimum = rows[0].reading;
  let maximum = rows[0].reading;
  for (const row of rows.slice(1)) {
    minimum = Math.min(minimum, row.reading);
    maximum = Math.max(maximum, row.reading);
  }
  return { count: rows.length, minimum, maximum, span: maximum - minimum };
}

===== FILE: modules/north_parcel_0306.py =====
"""Synthetic drift aggregation for the fictional north_parcel_0306 module."""

from dataclasses import dataclass


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Reading:
    label: str
    value: int
    accepted: bool


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Summary:
    accepted: int
    rejected: int
    total: int


def summarize_north_parcel_0306(readings: list[Reading], ceiling: int = 168) -> Summary:
    """Classify bounded mock readings without discarding rejected rows."""
    if ceiling < 1:
        raise ValueError("ceiling must be positive")
    accepted = 0
    rejected = 0
    total = 0
    for reading in readings:
        if reading.value < 0:
            raise ValueError("reading values must be non-negative")
        total += reading.value
        if reading.accepted and reading.value <= ceiling:
            accepted += 1
        else:
            rejected += 1
    return Summary(accepted=accepted, rejected=rejected, total=total)


def render_north_parcel_0306(summary: Summary) -> str:
    fields = (
        f"accepted={summary.accepted}",
        f"rejected={summary.rejected}",
        f"total={summary.total}",
    )
    return ", ".join(fields)

===== FILE: crates/tidal_ledger_0307/src/lib.rs =====
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Sample {
    pub sequence: u32,
    pub magnitude: u64,
    pub complete: bool,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Totals {
    pub complete: usize,
    pub incomplete: usize,
    pub magnitude: u64,
}

pub fn fold_tidal_ledger_0307(samples: &[Sample]) -> Result<Totals, &'static str> {
    if samples.len() > 456 {
        return Err("sample collection exceeds the synthetic bound");
    }
    let mut totals = Totals { complete: 0, incomplete: 0, magnitude: 0 };
    for sample in samples {
        totals.magnitude = totals.magnitude
            .checked_add(sample.magnitude)
            .ok_or("magnitude overflow")?;
        if sample.complete {
            totals.complete += 1;
        } else {
            totals.incomplete += 1;
        }
    }
    Ok(totals)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn incomplete_samples_remain_in_totals() {
        let samples = [
            Sample { sequence: 1, magnitude: 8, complete: true },
            Sample { sequence: 2, magnitude: 13, complete: false },
        ];
        let totals = fold_tidal_ledger_0307(&samples).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(totals.complete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.incomplete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.magnitude, 21);
    }
}

===== FILE: crates/evening_quarry_0316/src/lib.rs =====
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Sample {
    pub sequence: u32,
    pub magnitude: u64,
    pub complete: bool,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Totals {
    pub complete: usize,
    pub incomplete: usize,
    pub magnitude: u64,
}

pub fn fold_evening_quarry_0316(samples: &[Sample]) -> Result<Totals, &'static str> {
    if samples.len() > 186 {
        return Err("sample collection exceeds the synthetic bound");
    }
    let mut totals = Totals { complete: 0, incomplete: 0, magnitude: 0 };
    for sample in samples {
        totals.magnitude = totals.magnitude
            .checked_add(sample.magnitude)
            .ok_or("magnitude overflow")?;
        if sample.complete {
            totals.complete += 1;
        } else {
            totals.incomplete += 1;
        }
    }
    Ok(totals)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn incomplete_samples_remain_in_totals() {
        let samples = [
            Sample { sequence: 1, magnitude: 8, complete: true },
            Sample { sequence: 2, magnitude: 13, complete: false },
        ];
        let totals = fold_evening_quarry_0316(&samples).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(totals.complete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.incomplete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.magnitude, 21);
    }
}

===== FILE: web/indigo_yardstick_0317.ts =====
export type Observation = Readonly<{
  sequence: number;
  category: "calm" | "changing" | "uncertain";
  reading: number;
}>;

export type WindowSummary = Readonly<{
  count: number;
  minimum: number | null;
  maximum: number | null;
  span: number | null;
}>;

export function summarizeIndigoYardstick0317(
  rows: readonly Observation[],
): WindowSummary {
  if (rows.length === 0) {
    return { count: 0, minimum: null, maximum: null, span: null };
  }
  if (rows.length > 288) {
    throw new Error("synthetic observation window is too wide");
  }
  let minimum = rows[0].reading;
  let maximum = rows[0].reading;
  for (const row of rows.slice(1)) {
    minimum = Math.min(minimum, row.reading);
    maximum = Math.max(maximum, row.reading);
  }
  return { count: rows.length, minimum, maximum, span: maximum - minimum };
}

===== FILE: modules/granite_workshop_0324.py =====
"""Synthetic stability aggregation for the fictional granite_workshop_0324 module."""

from dataclasses import dataclass


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Reading:
    label: str
    value: int
    accepted: bool


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Summary:
    accepted: int
    rejected: int
    total: int


def summarize_granite_workshop_0324(readings: list[Reading], ceiling: int = 56) -> Summary:
    """Classify bounded mock readings without discarding rejected rows."""
    if ceiling < 1:
        raise ValueError("ceiling must be positive")
    accepted = 0
    rejected = 0
    total = 0
    for reading in readings:
        if reading.value < 0:
            raise ValueError("reading values must be non-negative")
        total += reading.value
        if reading.accepted and reading.value <= ceiling:
            accepted += 1
        else:
            rejected += 1
    return Summary(accepted=accepted, rejected=rejected, total=total)


def render_granite_workshop_0324(summary: Summary) -> str:
    fields = (
        f"accepted={summary.accepted}",
        f"rejected={summary.rejected}",
        f"total={summary.total}",
    )
    return ", ".join(fields)

===== FILE: web/granite_workshop_0326.ts =====
export type Observation = Readonly<{
  sequence: number;
  category: "calm" | "changing" | "uncertain";
  reading: number;
}>;

export type WindowSummary = Readonly<{
  count: number;
  minimum: number | null;
  maximum: number | null;
  span: number | null;
}>;

export function summarizeGraniteWorkshop0326(
  rows: readonly Observation[],
): WindowSummary {
  if (rows.length === 0) {
    return { count: 0, minimum: null, maximum: null, span: null };
  }
  if (rows.length > 384) {
    throw new Error("synthetic observation window is too wide");
  }
  let minimum = rows[0].reading;
  let maximum = rows[0].reading;
  for (const row of rows.slice(1)) {
    minimum = Math.min(minimum, row.reading);
    maximum = Math.max(maximum, row.reading);
  }
  return { count: rows.length, minimum, maximum, span: maximum - minimum };
}

name = "amber_index_0327"

===== FILE: crates/north_registry_0334/src/lib.rs =====
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Sample {
    pub sequence: u32,
    pub magnitude: u64,
    pub complete: bool,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Totals {
    pub complete: usize,
    pub incomplete: usize,
    pub magnitude: u64,
}

pub fn fold_north_registry_0334(samples: &[Sample]) -> Result<Totals, &'static str> {
    if samples.len() > 265 {
        return Err("sample collection exceeds the synthetic bound");
    }
    let mut totals = Totals { complete: 0, incomplete: 0, magnitude: 0 };
    for sample in samples {
        totals.magnitude = totals.magnitude
            .checked_add(sample.magnitude)
            .ok_or("magnitude overflow")?;
        if sample.complete {
            totals.complete += 1;
        } else {
            totals.incomplete += 1;
        }
    }
    Ok(totals)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn incomplete_samples_remain_in_totals() {
        let samples = [
            Sample { sequence: 1, magnitude: 8, complete: true },
            Sample { sequence: 2, magnitude: 13, complete: false },
        ];
        let totals = fold_north_registry_0334(&samples).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(totals.complete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.incomplete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.magnitude, 21);
    }
}

===== FILE: modules/quiet_kiln_0342.py =====
"""Synthetic clarity aggregation for the fictional quiet_kiln_0342 module."""

from dataclasses import dataclass


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Reading:
    label: str
    value: int
    accepted: bool


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Summary:
    accepted: int
    rejected: int
    total: int


def summarize_quiet_kiln_0342(readings: list[Reading], ceiling: int = 61) -> Summary:
    """Classify bounded mock readings without discarding rejected rows."""
    if ceiling < 1:
        raise ValueError("ceiling must be positive")
    accepted = 0
    rejected = 0
    total = 0
    for reading in readings:
        if reading.value < 0:
            raise ValueError("reading values must be non-negative")
        total += reading.value
        if reading.accepted and reading.value <= ceiling:
            accepted += 1
        else:
            rejected += 1
    return Summary(accepted=accepted, rejected=rejected, total=total)


def render_quiet_kiln_0342(summary: Summary) -> str:
    fields = (
        f"accepted={summary.accepted}",
        f"rejected={summary.rejected}",
        f"total={summary.total}",
    )
    return ", ".join(fields)

===== FILE: web/fern_garden_0344.ts =====
export type Observation = Readonly<{
  sequence: number;
  category: "calm" | "changing" | "uncertain";
  reading: number;
}>;

export type WindowSummary = Readonly<{
  count: number;
  minimum: number | null;
  maximum: number | null;
  span: number | null;
}>;

export function summarizeFernGarden0344(
  rows: readonly Observation[],
): WindowSummary {
  if (rows.length === 0) {
    return { count: 0, minimum: null, maximum: null, span: null };
  }
  if (rows.length > 192) {
    throw new Error("synthetic observation window is too wide");
  }
  let minimum = rows[0].reading;
  let maximum = rows[0].reading;
  for (const row of rows.slice(1)) {
    minimum = Math.min(minimum, row.reading);
    maximum = Math.max(maximum, row.reading);
  }
  return { count: rows.length, minimum, maximum, span: maximum - minimum };
}
