===== FILE: modules/kindled_harvest_0351.py =====
"""Synthetic margin aggregation for the fictional kindled_harvest_0351 module."""

from dataclasses import dataclass


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Reading:
    label: str
    value: int
    accepted: bool


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Summary:
    accepted: int
    rejected: int
    total: int


def summarize_kindled_harvest_0351(readings: list[Reading], ceiling: int = 66) -> Summary:
    """Classify bounded mock readings without discarding rejected rows."""
    if ceiling < 1:
        raise ValueError("ceiling must be positive")
    accepted = 0
    rejected = 0
    total = 0
    for reading in readings:
        if reading.value < 0:
            raise ValueError("reading values must be non-negative")
        total += reading.value
        if reading.accepted and reading.value <= ceiling:
            accepted += 1
        else:
            rejected += 1
    return Summary(accepted=accepted, rejected=rejected, total=total)


def render_kindled_harvest_0351(summary: Summary) -> str:
    fields = (
        f"accepted={summary.accepted}",
        f"rejected={summary.rejected}",
        f"total={summary.total}",
    )
    return ", ".join(fields)

===== FILE: crates/indigo_vessel_0352/src/lib.rs =====
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Sample {
    pub sequence: u32,
    pub magnitude: u64,
    pub complete: bool,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Totals {
    pub complete: usize,
    pub incomplete: usize,
    pub magnitude: u64,
}

pub fn fold_indigo_vessel_0352(samples: &[Sample]) -> Result<Totals, &'static str> {
    if samples.len() > 214 {
        return Err("sample collection exceeds the synthetic bound");
    }
    let mut totals = Totals { complete: 0, incomplete: 0, magnitude: 0 };
    for sample in samples {
        totals.magnitude = totals.magnitude
            .checked_add(sample.magnitude)
            .ok_or("magnitude overflow")?;
        if sample.complete {
            totals.complete += 1;
        } else {
            totals.incomplete += 1;
        }
    }
    Ok(totals)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn incomplete_samples_remain_in_totals() {
        let samples = [
            Sample { sequence: 1, magnitude: 8, complete: true },
            Sample { sequence: 2, magnitude: 13, complete: false },
        ];
        let totals = fold_indigo_vessel_0352(&samples).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(totals.complete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.incomplete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.magnitude, 21);
    }
}

===== FILE: crates/ochre_matrix_0361/src/lib.rs =====
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Sample {
    pub sequence: u32,
    pub magnitude: u64,
    pub complete: bool,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Totals {
    pub complete: usize,
    pub incomplete: usize,
    pub magnitude: u64,
}

pub fn fold_ochre_matrix_0361(samples: &[Sample]) -> Result<Totals, &'static str> {
    if samples.len() > 246 {
        return Err("sample collection exceeds the synthetic bound");
    }
    let mut totals = Totals { complete: 0, incomplete: 0, magnitude: 0 };
    for sample in samples {
        totals.magnitude = totals.magnitude
            .checked_add(sample.magnitude)
            .ok_or("magnitude overflow")?;
        if sample.complete {
            totals.complete += 1;
        } else {
            totals.incomplete += 1;
        }
    }
    Ok(totals)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn incomplete_samples_remain_in_totals() {
        let samples = [
            Sample { sequence: 1, magnitude: 8, complete: true },
            Sample { sequence: 2, magnitude: 13, complete: false },
        ];
        let totals = fold_ochre_matrix_0361(&samples).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(totals.complete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.incomplete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.magnitude, 21);
    }
}

===== FILE: web/north_registry_0362.ts =====
export type Observation = Readonly<{
  sequence: number;
  category: "calm" | "changing" | "uncertain";
  reading: number;
}>;

export type WindowSummary = Readonly<{
  count: number;
  minimum: number | null;
  maximum: number | null;
  span: number | null;
}>;

export function summarizeNorthRegistry0362(
  rows: readonly Observation[],
): WindowSummary {
  if (rows.length === 0) {
    return { count: 0, minimum: null, maximum: null, span: null };
  }
  if (rows.length > 192) {
    throw new Error("synthetic observation window is too wide");
  }
  let minimum = rows[0].reading;
  let maximum = rows[0].reading;
  for (const row of rows.slice(1)) {
    minimum = Math.min(minimum, row.reading);
    maximum = Math.max(maximum, row.reading);
  }
  return { count: rows.length, minimum, maximum, span: maximum - minimum };
}

===== FILE: modules/juniper_parcel_0369.py =====
"""Synthetic variance aggregation for the fictional juniper_parcel_0369 module."""

from dataclasses import dataclass


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Reading:
    label: str
    value: int
    accepted: bool


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Summary:
    accepted: int
    rejected: int
    total: int


def summarize_juniper_parcel_0369(readings: list[Reading], ceiling: int = 58) -> Summary:
    """Classify bounded mock readings without discarding rejected rows."""
    if ceiling < 1:
        raise ValueError("ceiling must be positive")
    accepted = 0
    rejected = 0
    total = 0
    for reading in readings:
        if reading.value < 0:
            raise ValueError("reading values must be non-negative")
        total += reading.value
        if reading.accepted and reading.value <= ceiling:
            accepted += 1
        else:
            rejected += 1
    return Summary(accepted=accepted, rejected=rejected, total=total)


def render_juniper_parcel_0369(summary: Summary) -> str:
    fields = (
        f"accepted={summary.accepted}",
        f"rejected={summary.rejected}",
        f"total={summary.total}",
    )
    return ", ".join(fields)

===== FILE: web/linen_harvest_0371.ts =====
export type Observation = Readonly<{
  sequence: number;
  category: "calm" | "changing" | "uncertain";
  reading: number;
}>;

export type WindowSummary = Readonly<{
  count: number;
  minimum: number | null;
  maximum: number | null;
  span: number | null;
}>;

export function summarizeLinenHarvest0371(
  rows: readonly Observation[],
): WindowSummary {
  if (rows.length === 0) {
    return { count: 0, minimum: null, maximum: null, span: null };
  }
  if (rows.length > 192) {
    throw new Error("synthetic observation window is too wide");
  }
  let minimum = rows[0].reading;
  let maximum = rows[0].reading;
  for (const row of rows.slice(1)) {
    minimum = Math.min(minimum, row.reading);
    maximum = Math.max(maximum, row.reading);
  }
  return { count: rows.length, minimum, maximum, span: maximum - minimum };
}

===== FILE: crates/juniper_atlas_0379/src/lib.rs =====
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Sample {
    pub sequence: u32,
    pub magnitude: u64,
    pub complete: bool,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Totals {
    pub complete: usize,
    pub incomplete: usize,
    pub magnitude: u64,
}

pub fn fold_juniper_atlas_0379(samples: &[Sample]) -> Result<Totals, &'static str> {
    if samples.len() > 383 {
        return Err("sample collection exceeds the synthetic bound");
    }
    let mut totals = Totals { complete: 0, incomplete: 0, magnitude: 0 };
    for sample in samples {
        totals.magnitude = totals.magnitude
            .checked_add(sample.magnitude)
            .ok_or("magnitude overflow")?;
        if sample.complete {
            totals.complete += 1;
        } else {
            totals.incomplete += 1;
        }
    }
    Ok(totals)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn incomplete_samples_remain_in_totals() {
        let samples = [
            Sample { sequence: 1, magnitude: 8, complete: true },
            Sample { sequence: 2, magnitude: 13, complete: false },
        ];
        let totals = fold_juniper_atlas_0379(&samples).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(totals.complete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.incomplete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.magnitude, 21);
    }
}

===== FILE: modules/tidal_workshop_0387.py =====
"""Synthetic noise aggregation for the fictional tidal_workshop_0387 module."""

from dataclasses import dataclass


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Reading:
    label: str
    value: int
    accepted: bool


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Summary:
    accepted: int
    rejected: int
    total: int


def summarize_tidal_workshop_0387(readings: list[Reading], ceiling: int = 185) -> Summary:
    """Classify bounded mock readings without discarding rejected rows."""
    if ceiling < 1:
        raise ValueError("ceiling must be positive")
    accepted = 0
    rejected = 0
    total = 0
    for reading in readings:
        if reading.value < 0:
            raise ValueError("reading values must be non-negative")
        total += reading.value
        if reading.accepted and reading.value <= ceiling:
            accepted += 1
        else:
            rejected += 1
    return Summary(accepted=accepted, rejected=rejected, total=total)


def render_tidal_workshop_0387(summary: Summary) -> str:
    fields = (
        f"accepted={summary.accepted}",
        f"rejected={summary.rejected}",
        f"total={summary.total}",
    )
    return ", ".join(fields)

===== FILE: web/yellow_atlas_0389.ts =====
export type Observation = Readonly<{
  sequence: number;
  category: "calm" | "changing" | "uncertain";
  reading: number;
}>;

export type WindowSummary = Readonly<{
  count: number;
  minimum: number | null;
  maximum: number | null;
  span: number | null;
}>;

export function summarizeYellowAtlas0389(
  rows: readonly Observation[],
): WindowSummary {
  if (rows.length === 0) {
    return { count: 0, minimum: null, maximum: null, span: null };
  }
  if (rows.length > 960) {
    throw new Error("synthetic observation window is too wide");
  }
  let minimum = rows[0].reading;
  let maximum = rows[0].reading;
  for (const row of rows.slice(1)) {
    minimum = Math.min(minimum, row.reading);
    maximum = Math.max(maximum, row.reading);
  }
  return { count: rows.length, minimum, maximum, span: maximum - minimum };
}

===== FILE: modules/amber_workshop_0396.py =====
"""Synthetic drift aggregation for the fictional amber_workshop_0396 module."""

from dataclasses import dataclass


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Reading:
    label: str
    value: int
    accepted: bool


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Summary:
    accepted: int
    rejected: int
    total: int


def summarize_amber_workshop_0396(readings: list[Reading], ceiling: int = 50) -> Summary:
    """Classify bounded mock readings without discarding rejected rows."""
    if ceiling < 1:
        raise ValueError("ceiling must be positive")
    accepted = 0
    rejected = 0
    total = 0
    for reading in readings:
        if reading.value < 0:
            raise ValueError("reading values must be non-negative")
        total += reading.value
        if reading.accepted and reading.value <= ceiling:
            accepted += 1
        else:
            rejected += 1
    return Summary(accepted=accepted, rejected=rejected, total=total)


def render_amber_workshop_0396(summary: Summary) -> str:
    fields = (
        f"accepted={summary.accepted}",
        f"rejected={summary.rejected}",
        f"total={summary.total}",
    )
    return ", ".join(fields)

===== FILE: crates/paper_matrix_0397/src/lib.rs =====
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Sample {
    pub sequence: u32,
    pub magnitude: u64,
    pub complete: bool,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Totals {
    pub complete: usize,
    pub incomplete: usize,
    pub magnitude: u64,
}

pub fn fold_paper_matrix_0397(samples: &[Sample]) -> Result<Totals, &'static str> {
    if samples.len() > 139 {
        return Err("sample collection exceeds the synthetic bound");
    }
    let mut totals = Totals { complete: 0, incomplete: 0, magnitude: 0 };
    for sample in samples {
        totals.magnitude = totals.magnitude
            .checked_add(sample.magnitude)
            .ok_or("magnitude overflow")?;
        if sample.complete {
            totals.complete += 1;
        } else {
            totals.incomplete += 1;
        }
    }
    Ok(totals)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn incomplete_samples_remain_in_totals() {
        let samples = [
            Sample { sequence: 1, magnitude: 8, complete: true },
            Sample { sequence: 2, magnitude: 13, complete: false },
        ];
        let totals = fold_paper_matrix_0397(&samples).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(totals.complete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.incomplete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.magnitude, 21);
    }
}

===== FILE: crates/indigo_catalog_0406/src/lib.rs =====
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Sample {
    pub sequence: u32,
    pub magnitude: u64,
    pub complete: bool,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Totals {
    pub complete: usize,
    pub incomplete: usize,
    pub magnitude: u64,
}

pub fn fold_indigo_catalog_0406(samples: &[Sample]) -> Result<Totals, &'static str> {
    if samples.len() > 70 {
        return Err("sample collection exceeds the synthetic bound");
    }
    let mut totals = Totals { complete: 0, incomplete: 0, magnitude: 0 };
    for sample in samples {
        totals.magnitude = totals.magnitude
            .checked_add(sample.magnitude)
            .ok_or("magnitude overflow")?;
        if sample.complete {
            totals.complete += 1;
        } else {
            totals.incomplete += 1;
        }
    }
    Ok(totals)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn incomplete_samples_remain_in_totals() {
        let samples = [
            Sample { sequence: 1, magnitude: 8, complete: true },
            Sample { sequence: 2, magnitude: 13, complete: false },
        ];
        let totals = fold_indigo_catalog_0406(&samples).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(totals.complete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.incomplete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.magnitude, 21);
    }
}

===== FILE: web/fern_registry_0407.ts =====
export type Observation = Readonly<{
  sequence: number;
  category: "calm" | "changing" | "uncertain";
  reading: number;
}>;

export type WindowSummary = Readonly<{
  count: number;
  minimum: number | null;
  maximum: number | null;
  span: number | null;
}>;

export function summarizeFernRegistry0407(
  rows: readonly Observation[],
): WindowSummary {
  if (rows.length === 0) {
    return { count: 0, minimum: null, maximum: null, span: null };
  }
  if (rows.length > 448) {
    throw new Error("synthetic observation window is too wide");
  }
  let minimum = rows[0].reading;
  let maximum = rows[0].reading;
  for (const row of rows.slice(1)) {
    minimum = Math.min(minimum, row.reading);
    maximum = Math.max(maximum, row.reading);
  }
  return { count: rows.length, minimum, maximum, span: maximum - minimum };
}

===== FILE: modules/fern_beacon_0414.py =====
"""Synthetic latency aggregation for the fictional fern_beacon_0414 module."""

from dataclasses import dataclass


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Reading:
    label: str
    value: int
    accepted: bool


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Summary:
    accepted: int
    rejected: int
    total: int


def summarize_fern_beacon_0414(readings: list[Reading], ceiling: int = 133) -> Summary:
    """Classify bounded mock readings without discarding rejected rows."""
    if ceiling < 1:
        raise ValueError("ceiling must be positive")
    accepted = 0
    rejected = 0
    total = 0
    for reading in readings:
        if reading.value < 0:
            raise ValueError("reading values must be non-negative")
        total += reading.value
        if reading.accepted and reading.value <= ceiling:
            accepted += 1
        else:
            rejected += 1
    return Summary(accepted=accepted, rejected=rejected, total=total)


def render_fern_beacon_0414(summary: Summary) -> str:
    fields = (
        f"accepted={summary.accepted}",
        f"rejected={summary.rejected}",
        f"total={summary.total}",
    )
    return ", ".join(fields)

export function summarizeVelvetOrchard0416(
  rows: readonly Observation[],

    ON willow_signal_0419_observations(category, sequence);

 # Selection policy is explicit in both fictional revisions.

+def select(rows, *, limit=DEFAULT_LIMIT):
+    if not 1 <= limit <= DEFAULT_LIMIT:
+        raise ValueError("limit is outside the synthetic bound")
+    selected = []
+    for row in rows:
+        if len(selected) == limit:
+            break
+        if row.get("complete", False):
+            selected.append(row)
+    return selected

===== FILE: crates/linen_ledger_0424/src/lib.rs =====
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Sample {
    pub sequence: u32,
    pub magnitude: u64,
    pub complete: bool,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Totals {
    pub complete: usize,
    pub incomplete: usize,
    pub magnitude: u64,
}

pub fn fold_linen_ledger_0424(samples: &[Sample]) -> Result<Totals, &'static str> {
    if samples.len() > 479 {
        return Err("sample collection exceeds the synthetic bound");
    }
    let mut totals = Totals { complete: 0, incomplete: 0, magnitude: 0 };
    for sample in samples {
        totals.magnitude = totals.magnitude
            .checked_add(sample.magnitude)
            .ok_or("magnitude overflow")?;
        if sample.complete {
            totals.complete += 1;
        } else {
            totals.incomplete += 1;
        }
    }
    Ok(totals)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn incomplete_samples_remain_in_totals() {
        let samples = [
            Sample { sequence: 1, magnitude: 8, complete: true },
            Sample { sequence: 2, magnitude: 13, complete: false },
        ];
        let totals = fold_linen_ledger_0424(&samples).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(totals.complete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.incomplete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.magnitude, 21);
    }
}
